CREATE OR REPLACE package Body PMS_GEN_PKG as
FUNCTION FUN_GET_PROJECT_DESC(V_PJ_NO VARCHAR2 , V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_PROJECT_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
BEGIN
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,PJ_A_NAME,PJ_E_NAME)
INTO V_PROJECT_DESC
FROM IAS_PROJECTS
WHERE PJ_NO = V_PJ_NO ;
RETURN(V_PROJECT_DESC);
EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
RETURN(NULL);
WHEN OTHERS THEN
END;
------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION FUN_GET_CNTRY_DESC(V_CNTRY_NO NUMBER , V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_CNTRY_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,CNTRY_A_NAME,CNTRY_E_NAME)
INTO V_CNTRY_DESC
FROM CNTRY
WHERE CNT
RY_NO = V_CNTRY_NO ;
RETURN(V_CNTRY_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_PROV_DESC(V_PROV_NO NUMBER , V_CNTRY_NO NUMBER,V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_PROV_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,PROV_A_NAME,PROV_E_NAME)
INTO V_PROV_DESC
FROM IAS_PROVINCES
WHERE PROV_NO = V_PROV_NO
AND CNTRY_NO = V_CNTRY_NO ;
RETURN(V_PROV_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_CITY_DESC(V_CITY_NO NUMBER ,V_PROV_NO NUMBER , V_CNTRY_NO NUMBER,V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_CITY_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,CITY_A_NAME,CITY_E_NAME)
INTO V_CITY_DESC
FROM CITIES
WHERE CITY_NO = V_CITY_NO
AND PROV_NO = V_PROV_NO
RETURN(V_CITY_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_R_CODE_DESC(V_R NUMBER , V_CY NUMBER ,V_P NUMBER , V_C NUMBER,V_L NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_R_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_L,1,R_A_NAME,R_E_NAME)
INTO V_R_DESC
FROM REGIONS
WHERE R_CODE = V_R
AND CITY_NO = V_CY
AND PROV_NO = V_P
AND CNTRY_NO = V_C ;
RETURN(V_R_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_C_CODE_DESC(V_C_CODE VARCHAR2 , V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_C_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,C_A_NAME,C_E_NAME)
INTO V_C_DESC
FROM CUSTOMER
WHERE C_CODE = V_C_CODE ;
RETURN(V_C_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_V_CODE_DESC(V_V_CODE VARCHAR2 , V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_V_DESC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,V_A_NAME,V_E_NAME)
INTO V_V_DESC
FROM V_DETAILS
WHERE V_CODE = V_V_CODE ;
RETURN(V_V_DESC);
FUNCTION FUN_GET_COD_NO_PJ_DESC(V_COD_NO_PJ NUMBER , V_COD_TYP NUMBER , V_LANG_NO NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_COD_DESC VARCHAR2(500);
SELECT DECODE(V_LANG_NO,1,COD_L_NM,COD_F_NM)
INTO V_COD_DESC
WHERE COD_NO = V_COD_NO_PJ
AND COD_TYP = V_COD_TYP ;
RETURN(V_COD_DESC);
PROCEDURE PRO_GET_PROJECT_CNTRCT_ITMS(V_CONTRACTS_DTL OUT PMS_CONTRACTS_DTL%ROWTYPE , V_DOC_SER NUMBER , V_ITEM_COD VARCHAR2) IS
SELECT DOC_SER ,
CNTRCT_NO ,
CNTRCT_TYPE ,

FUNCTION FUN_GET_NET_CNTRCT_TOT(V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT NUMBER := 0 ;
V_ADDING_AMT NUMBER := 0 ;
V_DEDUCTION_AMT NUMBER := 0 ;
SELECT NVL(CNTRCT_TOT_AMT,0)
INTO V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT
WHERE DOC_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER ;
----------------------------------------------------
SELECT NVL(SUM(CURR_ADDING_AMT),0) ,
NVL(SUM(CURR_DEDUCTION_AMT),0)
INTO V_ADDING_AMT ,
V_DEDUCTION_AMT
FROM PMS_CONTRACTS_QTY_MST
WHERE DOC_CNTRCT_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER
AND SUB_CNTRCT_SER IS NULL ;
RETURN ((V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT + V_ADDING_AMT ) - V_DEDUCTION_AMT) ;
EXCEPTION
when no_data_found then
RETURN(V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT) ;
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION FUN_GET_NET_SCNTRCT_TOT(V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER IS
SELECT NVL(SUB_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT,0)
FROM PMS_SUB_CONTRACTS_MST
WHERE SUB_CNTRCT_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER;

AND DOC_CNTRCT_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER);

FUNCTION FUN_GET_TOT_COST3(V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER NUMBER , V_ITEM_COD VARCHAR2) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_TOT_QTY NUMBER := 0 ;
V_TOT_AMT NUMBER := 0 ;
V_ALL_QTY_TOT NUMBER := 0 ;
CURSOR COST_DTL IS
SELECT ITEM_COD , UNIT_PRICE
WHERE DOC_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER
AND ITEM_FLG = 3
AND QTY_AMT_FLG = 2
AND ITEM_COD_PARENT = V_ITEM_COD
FOR I IN COST_DTL LOOP
SELECT NVL(SUM(net_qty),0)
INTO V_TOT_QTY
FROM pms_v_contracts_QTY
AND ITEM_COD = I.ITEM_COD ;
V_TOT_QTY := V_TOT_QTY * I.UNIT_PRICE ;
V_ALL_QTY_TOT := V_ALL_QTY_TOT + V_TOT_QTY ;
V_TOT_QTY := 0 ;
END LOOP ;
INTO V_TOT_AMT
FROM pms_v_contracts_AMTS
AND ITEM_COD IN (SELECT ITEM_COD FROM PMS_CONTRACTS_DTL WHERE DOC_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER AND ITEM_COD_PARENT = V_ITEM_COD
AND ITEM_FLG = 3 AND QTY_AMT_FLG = 1) ;
RETURN(V_ALL_QTY_TOT + V_TOT_AMT) ;
FUNCTION FUN_SUM_COST_4(V_PJ_NO VARCHAR2 , V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER NUMBER , V_ITEM_COD_PARENT NUMBER , V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY OUT NUMBER , V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY OUT NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER IS
SELECT A.ITEM_COD , NVL(SUM(ACHVE_NOW_QTY),0) ACHVE_NOW_QTY , NVL(MAX(ACHVE_ALL_QTY),0) ACHVE_ALL_QTY
FROM PMS_ACHVE_JOBS_DTL A , PMS_CONTRACTS_DTL B
WHERE A.DOC_SER IN (SELECT DOC_SER FROM PMS_ACHVE_JOBS_MST
AND DOC_CNTRCT_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER)
AND B.ITEM_COD = A.ITEM_COD
AND B.ITEM_COD_PARENT = V_ITEM_COD_PARENT
AND B.ITEM_FLG = 3
AND B.DOC_SER = V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER
AND B.PJ_NO = V_PJ_NO
GROUP BY A.ITEM_COD ;
V_QTY_AMT_FLG NUMBER := 2 ;
V_UNIT_PRICE NUMBER := 0 ;
V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY :
= 0;
V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY := 0;
SELECT QTY_AMT_FLG ,
UNIT_PRICE
INTO V_QTY_AMT_FLG ,
V_UNIT_PRICE
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
IF V_QTY_AMT_FLG = 1 THEN
V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY := V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY + I.ACHVE_NOW_QTY;
V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY := V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY + I.ACHVE_ALL_QTY;
ELSE
V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY := V_ACHVE_NOW_QTY + ( I.ACHVE_NOW_QTY * V_UNIT_PRICE ) ;
V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY := V_ACHVE_ALL_QTY + ( I.ACHVE_ALL_QTY * V_UNIT_PRICE ) ;
END IF ;
RETURN(1) ;
----------------------------------------------------------------

FUNCTION Fun_GET_NET_AMT_FLG(V_DOC_CNTRCT_SER NUMBER,V_ITEM_COD VARCHAR2) RETURN NUMBER IS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FUNCTION Fun_GET_Cntrct_NET_AMT(V_DOC_SER NUMBER , V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_ADDING_AMT NUMBER;
V_DEDUCTION_AMT NUMBER;
WHERE DOC_CNTRCT_SER = V_DOC_SER
RETURN((V_CNTRCT_TOT_AMT + V_ADDING_AMT ) - V_DEDUCTION_AMT) ;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P_ACTV_NO In IAS_ACTVTY.ACTV_NO%TYPE DEFAULT NULL) IS
V_QTY NUMBER := 0;
V_ITEM_QTY NUMBER := 0;
----------------------------
V_CNT NUMBER(8) := 0;
V_COND_NO NUMBER ;
V_PRJCT_COST_FLG NUMBER(1):= 1;
-- 0 NOT ALLAWED
-- 1 ALLAWED
-- 2 ALLAWED WITH IN THE THE EXCEED PERCENTAGE.
P_Msg_No := NULL ;
P_EXCD_TYP := 1 ;
V_COND_NO := FUN_GET_PRJ_COND_NO(P_PJ_NO);
V_PRJCT_COST_FLG := Fun_GET_PROJECTS_COST_TYP(V_COND_NO ) ;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
IF P_PJ_NO IS NOT NULL AND P_I_CODE IS NOT NULL AND P_ACTV_NO IS NOT NULL AND V_PRJCT_COST_FLG IN (3,4)THEN
SELECT NVL(COUNT(ITEM_COD),0)
INTO V_CNT
WHERE PJ_NO = P_PJ_NO
AND I_CODE = P_I_CODE
AND ACTV_NO = P_ACTV_NO
AND ROWNUM<=1 ;
IF V_CNT > 0 THEN
SELECT SUM(QTY)
INTO V_ITEM_QTY
AND ACTV_NO = P_ACT
V_NO ;
------------------------------------------
SELECT NVL(SUM(P_QTY),0)
INTO V_QTY
FROM PMS_V_PJ_ITM_QTY
AND DOC_TYP = P_TRN_TYP ;
--==========================================
V_QTY := NVL(V_QTY,0) + NVL(P_P_QTY,0) ;
--==========================================================================
IF V_QTY > V_ITEM_QTY THEN
P_Msg_No := 3224;
P_Msg_No := NULL;
P_Msg_No := 3187;
Exception
When Others Then
P_Msg_No :=19;
P_EXCD_TYP :=1 ;
END Chk_Pj_Itm_Qty;

FUNCTION Get_Value_Fnc ( P_Fld_Nm Varchar2 , P_Tab_Nm In Varchar2 , P_Whr In Varchar2 ) RETURN Varchar2 IS
V_Fld_Value Varchar2(4000);
Begin
Execute Immediate ('Select '||P_Fld_Nm ||' From '|| P_Tab_Nm ||' '||P_Whr) Into V_Fld_Value ;
RETURN(V_Fld_Value) ;
End Get_Value_Fnc ;

END PRO_SET_PRJ_ST;

P_Usr_No In Pls_Integer) Is

FUNCTION Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc ( P_Doc_Typ In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_TYPE%TYPE , P_Doc_Srl In IAS_POST_MST.DOC_SER%TYPE) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_cnt NUMBER:=0;
Select 1
Into V_cnt
From IAS_POST_MST
Where DOC_TYPE = P_Doc_Typ
And DOC_SER = P_Doc_Srl
And RowNum<=1;
RETURN (V_cnt);
RETURN (0);
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR (-20442,'Error When Select IAS_POST_MST '||SqlErrm);
END Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc;
FUNCTION GET_PMS_TRN_PROJECT_ACCNTS ( P_PJ_NO In IAS_PROJECTS.PJ_NO%TYPE , P_COD_NO In PMS_COD_PROJECTS_CODS.COD_NO%TYPE) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_A_CODE VARCHAR2(30);
Select A_CODE
Into V_A_CODE
From PMS_TRN_PROJECT_ACCNTS
Where PJ_NO = P_PJ_NO
And COD_NO = P_COD_NO
And COD_TYP = 7 ;
RETURN (V_A_CODE);
RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR (-20442,'Error When Select PMS_TRN_PROJECT_ACCNTS '||SqlErrm)
END GET_PMS_TRN_PROJECT_ACCNTS;

DELETE FROM IAS_POST_MST

p_a_cy => V_CUR_CODE ,

IF V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_2 IS NOT NULL AND M_cv.ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT > 0 THEN

ting_Pkg.Insrt_Post_Dtl(
p_a_code => V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_3 ,

IF NVL(V_CONTRACTS_MST.CNTRCT_POST_FLG,0) = 1 THEN

code (V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_4,V_CUR_CODE);

,(nvl(ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT,0) * nvl(IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_Rate(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER)),1)) ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT
,Decode(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER),G_loc_cur_Code,0,nvl(ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT,0)) ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT_F
,(nvl(ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT,0) * nvl(IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_Rate(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER)),1)) ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT
,Decode(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER),G_loc_cur_Code,0,nvl(ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT,0)) ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT_F
,(nvl(TOT_NET_AMT,0) * nvl(IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_Rate(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER)),1)) TOT_NET_AMT
,Decode(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER),G_loc_cur_Code,0,nvl(TOT_NET_AMT,0)) TOT_NET_AMT_F
,(nvl(JOBS_DONE_AMT,0) * nvl(IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cur_Rate(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER)),1)) JOBS_DONE_AMT
,Decode(PMS_GEN_PKG.FUN_GET_SCNTRCT_CUR_CODE(Mst.SUB_CNTRCT_SER),G_loc_cur_Code,0,nvl(JOBS_DONE_AMT,0)) JOBS_DONE_AMT_F
From PMS_S_FINCE_RECEIPT_MST Mst
IF Chk_Exist_Doc_Fnc (P_DOC_TYP => M_cv.Doc_Typ, P_DOC_SRL => M_cv.Doc_Srl ) = 0 THEN -- (2)

IF V_CONTRACTS_MST.V_CODE IS NOT NULL THEN
P_Ad_U_Id =
> M_cv.Ad_U_Id ,
p_doc_type => M_cv.doc_typ
V_PRJCT_LNKD_ACCNTS := PMS_GEN_PKG.Fun_GET_PROJECTS_COST_LNK(M_cv.PJ_NO);
IF V_PRJCT_LNKD_ACCNTS = 1 THEN
For D_cv In
( Select Dtl.COD_NO
, Dtl.A_CODE
From PMS_TRN_PROJECT_ACCNTS Dtl
Where Dtl.PJ_NO = M_cv.PJ_NO)
LOOP
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (D_cv.A_CODE,V_S_CUR_CODE);
SELECT NVL(SUM(JOBS_NOW_AMT),0)
INTO V_JOBS_COST_AMT
FROM PMS_S_FINCE_RECEIPT_DTL
WHERE DOC_SER = M_cv.Doc_Srl
AND ITEM_COD IN (SELECT ITEM_COD FROM PMS_CONTRACTS_DTL
WHERE DOC_SER = M_cv.DOC_CNTRCT_SER
AND ITEM_COST_TYPE = D_
cv.COD_NO);

p_a_code => D_cv.A_CODE ,
p_a_cy => V_S_CUR_CODE ,
p_amt => V_JOBS_COST_AMT ,
p_amt_f => V_JOBS_COST_AMT_F ,
P_Brn_No => M_Cv.Brn_No ,
V_JOBS_COST_AMT := 0 ;
ELSIF V_PRJCT_LNKD_ACCNTS = 2 THEN
( SELECT DISTINCT(A_CODE) A_CODE FROM PMS_CONTRACTS_DTL
WHERE DOC_SER = M_cv.DOC_CNTRCT_SER)
AND A_CODE = D_cv.A_CODE);
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_5,V_S_CUR_CODE);

p_amt_f => - (M_cv.TOT_NET_AMT_F),

p_amt_f => - (M_cv.TOT_NET_AMT_F) ,
P_V_CODE => V_CONTRACTS_MST.V_CODE);
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_1,V_S_CUR_CODE);
p_amt => - (M_cv.DEPOSIT_AMT) ,
p_amt_f => - (M_cv.DEPOSIT_AMT_F) ,

p_a_cy => V_S_CUR_CODE,
p_amt => - (M_cv.ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT) ,
p_amt_f => - (M_cv.ENSR_IMPLMNTN_AMT_F ) ,
IF V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_3 IS NOT NULL AND M_cv.ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT IS NOT NULL THEN
v_curr_blnc:=IAS_Posting_Pkg.Check_Acode (V_CONTRACTS_MST.A_CODE_3,V_S_CUR_CODE);
p_amt => - (M_cv.ENSR_END_IMPLMNTN_AMT) ,

When Others T

FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE

Into V_COD_NO

:NEW.COD_NO := V_COD_NO ;

ON PMS_ACHVE_JOBS_MST REFERENCING NEW AS New OLD AS Old

Into V_JOBS_NO
From PMS_ACHVE_JOBS_MST
WHERE PJ_NO = :NEW.PJ_NO ;
:NEW.JOBS_NO := V_JOBS_NO ;
:NEW.DOC_SER := lpad(:NEW.BRN_YEAR,4,0) || --BRN_YEAR
lpad(:NEW.BRN_NO,6,0) || --BRN_NO
SEQ_PMS_ACHVE_JOBS_MST.nextval || --PJ_NO
V_JOBS_NO ; ---CNTRCT_NO

+ NVL ((SELECT SUM (edit_qty)
unit_price

FROM pms_contracts_dtl a

CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW pms_v_pj_itm_qty (doc_typ,
i_name,
itm_unt,
w_code,
cc_code,
i_qty,
p_size,
p_qty,
in_out
SELECT 1 doc_typ, a.pj_no, m.i_code, m.i_name, itm_unt, w_code, cc_code , ACTV_NO ,
NVL (i_qty, 0) i_qty, d.p_size,
NVL (i_qty, 0) * NVL (p_size, 0) p_qty, 1 in_out
FROM ias_itm_mst m, ias_pi_bill_dtl d, ias_projects a -- (Purchase)
WHERE m.i_code = d.i_code AND d.pj_no = a.pj_no
UNION ALL
SELECT 2 doc_typ, a.pj_no, m.i_code, m.i_name, itm_unt, w_code, cc_code , ACTV_NO,
NVL (i_qty, 0) * NVL (p_size, 0) p_qty, -1 in_out
FROM ias_itm_mst m, ias_outgoing_dtl d, ias_projects a -- (Outgoing)
